Returns the linkedin_company_id needed for URL construction (content feeds, job searches, new hire monitoring, CURRENT_COMPANY/PAST_COMPANY filters). Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-company) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-company-affiliates**: Extract company affiliates (subsidiaries and parent companies) from LinkedIn via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to find subsidiaries, parent companies, related entities, or corporate structure of a company. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-company-affiliates) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-company-employees-insights**: Extract employee insights and headcount breakdown from a LinkedIn company page via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to see employee distribution by department, location, or school, analyze a company's workforce composition, or get headcount by function and seniority. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-company-employees-insights) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-connections**: Extract the full connections list from a LinkedIn identity's network via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to get all their LinkedIn connections, export their network, audit connection quality, or build a list of 1st-degree contacts. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). Supports incremental sync for monitoring new connections. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-connections) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-contact**: Extract contact information (email, phone, address) from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get someone's contact details from their LinkedIn profile. Requires a connected LinkedIn identity (`identity_ids`). Does not support managed mode. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-contact) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-conversations**: Extract LinkedIn messaging conversations via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to list their LinkedIn message threads, check for replies in outreach, detect who replied by checking last_message sender, or audit messaging activity. Also use for sync-before-send — always extract conversations immediately before sending a message. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-conversations) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-event**: Extract LinkedIn event details via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to get event information, see event description, organizer, date, or attendee count from a LinkedIn event URL. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-event) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-event-attendees**: Extract attendees registered for a LinkedIn event via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people attending a LinkedIn event, build an attendee list, or prospect from event registrations. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-event-attendees) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-followers**: Extract the list of people following a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to see who follows their profile, audit their follower base, or analyze follower quality. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). Supports incremental sync for monitoring new followers. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-followers) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-group-members**: Extract members of a LinkedIn group via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people in a LinkedIn group, prospect from a group, or analyze group membership. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-group-members) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-job**: Extract details of a LinkedIn job posting via the Edges API. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-job) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-licenses-certifications**: Extract licenses and certifications from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-messages**: Extract messages from a LinkedIn conversation thread via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to read messages in a specific LinkedIn conversation, get the full message history of a thread, or check message content. - **edges-linkedin-extract-page-followers**: Extract followers of a LinkedIn company page via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people following a company page, audit company page followers, or prospect from a company's audience. Requires admin access to the company page (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-page-followers) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people**: Extract full LinkedIn profile data via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a LinkedIn profile, enrich a person, extract profile data from a LinkedIn URL, look up someone on LinkedIn, get professional information about a person, or retrieve a profile's headline, experience, education, or skills. This is the primary profile extraction action — use extract-people-experiences for complete job history beyond the last 5, and extract-contact for visible email/phone on the profile. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-comment-activity**: Extract posts that a specific person COMMENTED ON via the Edges API. This returns posts where the person left a comment — their commenting activity, not comments on their posts. To get people who commented on a specific post, use edges-linkedin-extract-post-commenters instead. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-comment-activity) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-educations**: Extract education history from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to get someone's schools, degrees, fields of study, or education background. Cookieless (managed mode). Provides more detail than the education data returned by extract-people with sections: true. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-educations) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-experiences**: Extract complete work experience history from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user needs full job history, wants all positions beyond the last 5 returned by extract-people, or needs to analyze someone's complete career trajectory. Cookieless (managed mode). Note: linkedin-extract-people only returns the last 5 experiences — use this skill for complete history paginated in chunks of 20. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-experiences) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-post-activity**: Extract posts PUBLISHED BY a specific person on LinkedIn via the Edges API. This skill returns posts that the person authored — their own content feed. Do NOT use this for finding posts that mention someone — use edges-linkedin-search-content for that instead. Use when the user wants to see what someone has been posting, analyze their content strategy, or get their recent LinkedIn posts. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-post-activity) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-reaction-activity**: Extract posts that a specific person REACTED TO (liked) via the Edges API. This returns posts the person engaged with — their reaction history, not who reacted to their posts. To get people who liked a specific post, use edges-linkedin-extract-post-likers instead. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-reaction-activity) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-skills**: Extract the full skills list with endorsement counts from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants all skills for a person, needs endorsement data, or wants to analyze someone's technical competencies. Cookieless (managed mode). Note: linkedin-extract-people with skills: true only returns 1-2 main skills — use this skill for the complete list. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-skills) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-post**: Extract LinkedIn post details including content, engagement metrics, and author data via the Edges API. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-post) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-post-commenters**: Extract people who commented on a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-post-likers**: Extract people who liked a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. For full list without manual pagination, use async mode. Cookieless (managed mode). - **edges-linkedin-extract-post-reposters**: Extract people who reposted a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-post-reposters) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-profile-viewers**: Extract people who viewed your LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-like-post**: Like a LinkedIn post via the Edges API.
